# Household hh_s4 — 22 days, Monday to Monday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Day 4 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d4`: Hana hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:29–17:55).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d4`: Priya is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:21–15:51).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d4`: Dana is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:45–16:15).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_3:d4`: Hana is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:17–16:54).
- `ep:rough_day_at_work:resident_2:d4`: Dana had a rough day at work: goes quiet in the evening, keeps the phone close, the jacket lands on the couch (17:30–24:00).
- `distracted:resident_2`: Dana is distracted (0.73): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.

Internal states (0–1): Priya energy 0.80, hurriedness 0.56, distraction 0.66; Dana energy 0.42, hurriedness 0.59, distraction 0.73; Hana energy 0.27, hurriedness 0.41, distraction 0.40

## Day 10 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d10`: Priya is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:14–16:44).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d9:due_today`: Hana's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_3:d9:relief`: Hana made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d10`: Hana gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:50–21:35).
- `ep:rough_day_at_work:resident_2:d10`: Dana had a rough day at work: goes quiet in the evening, keeps the phone close, the jacket lands on the couch (17:30–24:00).
- `low_energy:resident_3`: Hana has low energy (0.20): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `running_late:resident_1`: Priya is running late all day (0.78): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Priya energy 0.63, hurriedness 0.78, distraction 0.55; Dana energy 0.59, hurriedness 0.50, distraction 0.31; Hana energy 0.20, hurriedness 0.52, distraction 0.31

- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d12`: Priya turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
